n=414 · ok 414 graded lines (59 taken, 355 passed shadows). Lean 1.00 = raw model; under 1 pulls prices toward 50-50. A market needs 8 graded lines before its lean moves. Where the backtest fit beat the raw model out of sample, that fit (k, drag) prices the live board; elsewhere the live lean is used. Untraded forecasts: 398 stored, 289 graded (calibration only).

BRIER OOS RAW0.2422
BRIER OOS CALIBRATED0.2417

optimistic Optimistic: every bet is priced at the closing line, and the closing line is also a model input. Cutoffs frozen 2026-10-18 (NFL 2025-12-21, NHL 2026-03-10, MLB 2026-08-07); new games only join the test set. Frozen for forward testing: NFL recent-form weight 0.75, no edge threshold; NHL/MLB margin anchored to the no-vig moneyline. The calibration on/off flag is chosen on the test set, so the test set is no longer fully clean. 12365 per-row predictions saved.
n=12254 graded lines 4130 settled NFL 2025-26, NHL 2025-26 and MLB 2026 games (plus the current NFL/NHL seasons to date) replayed through Clerk's eval.ts with rule-based inputs only: season-to-date scoring, last-5 form and rest rebuilt from games that finished before each game, priced against the pre-game closing line. No ESPN projection, injuries or betting splits (none are archived). Train = first 70% of each sport's games by date, test = last 30%. The fit is Clerk's own lean k and drag, searched in Clerk's ranges (k 0.45–1.15, drag 0–6). Lookahead risk: the "close" is the last pre-game number ESPN archived and may be captured at kickoff; in-game "Live Odds" are excluded.
